That's why I'm here, I guess.\n\nDoctor: When you were repeating those table numbers, did it feel like you were doing it because you were hearing voices telling you to, or were you just confused?\n\nPatient: Um... I don't really know about being confused. But... sometimes, like when I'm closing out the register... or just at home in my kitchen... I hear this woman. \n\nShe says my name, or... tells me I'm doing the count wrong. It's clear. I actually answer her out loud before I realize nobody else heard it. So... I guess it was that.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been hearing this woman's voice?\n\nPatient: Um... I guess... about four months. That's when she got a lot clearer. And... I don't know, that's also when I realized what my coworkers and the customers were doing. That they were... targeting me. But... yeah. About four months.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced any other physical symptoms lately, like unexplained headaches, seizures, or changes in your vision?\n\nPatient: Um... no. Not really. I haven't... I don't get seizures or anything like that. My eyes are fine.\n\nDoctor: Besides the voice and the feeling that you are being targeted, have you noticed any other changes in your life, like having trouble taking care of yourself or feeling like you have lost the ability to show emotions?\n\nPatient: Um... I don't know. I guess I just... I don't really do much anymore. \n\nLike... I used to go for walks on the beach, or bake... garden... write in my journal. I just... don't. And when my husband tries to talk to me, I mostly just sit there. It's not that I'm trying to be mean, it's just... I can't really get myself to care. Or start anything. So... yeah. Just that.\n\nDoctor: When you try to explain your thoughts to your husband, do you find that your words get jumbled, or that you have trouble keeping your sentences on track?\n\nPatient: Um... no. Not really. I don't... I mean, I don't really talk to him much anyway, so... I don't think so. My words are fine. I just... don't have much to say.\n\nDoctor: Can you tell me more about how you feel you are being targeted by your coworkers and customers?\n\nPatient: Um... I don't really like talking about work. But... it's just a couple of the other servers. And one of the regular customers. \n\nThey just... they watch me. Like, they watch exactly how I ring things in. And then they leave these... little signs on the tickets. Just to prove they're trying to get me fired. My husband says I'm wrong, that it's just... normal restaurant mess-ups, but... I don't know. I just know what they're doing. So... yeah.\n\nDoctor: Before these symptoms started four months ago, or at any point since, did you experience any prolonged periods where your mood was extremely low or, alternatively, unusually high and energetic?\n\nPatient: Um... no. Not really. I just... I haven't really felt like doing anything for a while now. But... no, nothing like that. Just... what I already told you.\n\nDoctor: When you are at home or at work, do people ever tell you that your behavior seems strange, disorganized, or hard to follow, aside from the times you were repeating table numbers?\n\nPatient: Um... I guess so. At work... they said I was acting weird. Like... I'd just stop moving. Right in the middle of a rush. Or I'd be... doing things wrong. Like, I'd just stand by the soda machine with the key for the cash drawer... and I wouldn't close out the checks. Just standing there... saying the table numbers over and over. That's... that's what freaked them out. But... I don't really know what else they thought was strange.\n\nDoctor: When you think about everything that has been happening, what do you feel is the main problem that is most affecting your life right now?\n\nPatient: Um... probably work. I'm... I might lose this shift-lead spot I was supposed to get. Just because of... the mistakes, and getting confused. They say I'm disrupting service and closing. \n\nAnd... I don't know. At home, my husband just says I'm... barely present with him anymore. So... I guess that's it.\n\nDoctor: Looking back further than four months ago, can you recall if you were experiencing any of these same feelings, like the voice or the belief that people were watching you, even if they were much milder at that time?\n\nPatient: Um... I don't know if it was the same things exactly. But... things have been different for a while. \n\nMaybe... a little over a year? Like a year and two months, I think. That's when they first asked me to start doing the evening shift lead stuff. I just... I haven't really been the same since then. It just started building up.\n\nDoctor: When you are speaking to your husband or coworkers, do they ever mention that your sentences don't make sense or that you seem to jump from one topic to another without a clear connection?\n\nPatient: Um... no. I don't think so. They haven't really said that to me. Just... what I already told you.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like the television, the radio, or things you see in public are specifically sending you secret messages or talking about you?\n\nPatient: Um... no. Not really. I just watch regular shows. They're just... normal. Nobody's sending me messages through the TV or anything like that.\n\nDoctor: When you are at work or at home, have you ever noticed that you have trouble keeping your thoughts together, or that you find yourself unable to move or speak for long periods of time?\n\nPatient: Um... yeah, I guess. I mean... I don't really know about the thoughts part, but... the moving part... yeah. \n\nAt work, they got... they got pretty freaked out. I guess I was just... stopping. Right in the middle of a shift. Like... I'd just be standing by the soda machine, holding the key for the register, and... I'd just be saying table numbers over and over. Instead of, you know, actually closing out the checks. I don't really know why. It just happened.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing.
